definition{
  name: wordLess,
  parameters: [left : UInt64, right : UInt64],
  result: Bool,
  body: (match (prim checked_subtract left right : (Option UInt64))
    | Option.none => true
    | Option.some difference => false),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

  body: (if (wordLess position (3 : UInt64)) then (if (wordLess position (1 : UInt64)) then (if (prim equal position (0 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.event (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Event.beginBlock (0 : UInt64)))) else (ctor Option.none [Atom])) else (if (wordLess position (2 : UInt64)) then (if (prim equal position (1 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.event (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Event.beginBody (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::RuntimeType.sequence) (1 : UInt64)))) else (ctor Option.none [Atom])) else (if (prim equal position (2 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.event (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Event.beginInstruction (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeOpcode::Opcode.sequenceConstant)))) else (ctor Option.none [Atom])))) else (if (wordLess position (5 : UInt64)) then (if (wordLess position (4 : UInt64)) then (if (prim equal position (3 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.literal payload)) else (ctor Option.none [Atom])) else (if (prim equal position (4 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.event (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Event.endInstruction))) else (ctor Option.none [Atom]))) else (if (wordLess position (6 : UInt64)) then (if (prim equal position (5 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.event (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Event.endBlock (1 : UInt64)))) else (ctor Option.none [Atom])) else (if (prim equal position (6 : UInt64) : Bool) then (ctor Option.some [Atom] (ctor Atom.event (ctor Uorc.Core.RuntimeProgram::Event.seal (0 : UInt64)))) else (ctor Option.none [Atom]))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: eventCount,
  parameters: [template : Template],
  result: UInt64,
  body: (match template
    | Template.affine count base slope => (44 : UInt64)
    | Template.state count initial multiplier increment => (52 : UInt64)
    | Template.stateFold count initial multiplier increment => (48 : UInt64)
    | Template.dictionary count width codeBytes codes dictionary tail => (103 : UInt64)
    | Template.packed count width bits codes dictionary tail => (118 : UInt64)
    | Template.packedSingleton count width dictionary tail => (37 : UInt64)
    | Template.huffman count bitCount depth root codes tree => (1102 : UInt64)
    | Template.huffmanSingleton count symbol => (19 : UInt64)
    | Template.empty => (7 : UInt64)
    | Template.literalSequence payload => (7 : UInt64))
}

definition{
  name: at,
  parameters: [cursor : Cursor],
  result: (Option Atom),
  body: (match (project cursor template)
    | Template.affine count base slope => (affineAt (project cursor position) count base slope)
    | Template.state count initial multiplier increment => (stateAt (project cursor position) count initial multiplier increment)
    | Template.stateFold count initial multiplier increment => (stateFoldAt (project cursor position) count initial multiplier increment)
    | Template.dictionary count width codeBytes codes dictionary tail => (dictionaryAt (project cursor position) count width codeBytes codes dictionary tail)
    | Template.packed count width bits codes dictionary tail => (packedAt (project cursor position) count width bits codes dictionary tail)
    | Template.packedSingleton count width dictionary tail => (packedSingletonAt (project cursor position) count width dictionary tail)
    | Template.huffman count bitCount depth root codes tree => (huffmanAt (project cursor position) count bitCount depth root codes tree)
    | Template.huffmanSingleton count symbol => (huffmanSingletonAt (project cursor position) count symbol)
    | Template.empty => (emptyAt (project cursor position))
    | Template.literalSequence payload => (literalSequenceAt (project cursor position) payload)),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: step,
  parameters: [cursor : Cursor],
  result: EventStep,
  body: (if (prim equal (project cursor position) (eventCount (project cursor template)) : Bool) then (ctor EventStep.complete) else (match (at cursor)
    | Option.none => (ctor EventStep.invalidPosition)
    | Option.some offered => (match (prim checked_add (project cursor position) (1 : UInt64) : (Option UInt64))
      | Option.none => (ctor EventStep.invalidPosition)
      | Option.some following => (match offered
        | Atom.event semanticEvent => (ctor EventStep.produced semanticEvent (record Cursor {template := (project cursor template), position := following}))
        | Atom.literal sequenceRoot => (ctor EventStep.literal sequenceRoot (record Cursor {template := (project cursor template), position := following})))))),
  axioms: [Quot.sound, propext]
}

definition{
  name: ownedSequences,
  parameters: [template : Template],
  result: (List Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle),
  body: (match template
    | Template.affine count base slope => (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)
    | Template.state count initial multiplier increment => (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)
    | Template.stateFold count initial multiplier increment => (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)
    | Template.dictionary count width codeBytes codes dictionary tail => (cons codes (cons dictionary (cons tail (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle))))
    | Template.packed count width bits codes dictionary tail => (cons codes (cons dictionary (cons tail (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle))))
    | Template.packedSingleton count width dictionary tail => (cons dictionary (cons tail (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)))
    | Template.huffman count bitCount depth root codes tree => (cons codes (cons tree (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)))
    | Template.huffmanSingleton count symbol => (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)
    | Template.empty => (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)
    | Template.literalSequence payload => (cons payload (nil Uorc.Core.RuntimeValue::Handle)))
}

definition{
  name: contract,
  parameters: [],
  result: String,
  body: "Fixed-template discovery emits RuntimeProgram.Event directly, with no retained Graph, Block, Instruction, Bytes, Rope, or payload list. Literal atoms designate existing charged logical sequence roots. A literal consumer derives its actual length from the arena, emits beginLiteral, and traverses any number of RuntimeSequence image spans before resuming static syntax. Each nonempty terminal span transfers a temporary owned image root ticket, held through full admission acknowledgment and then explicitly released. No contiguous-image coalescing or payload-size cap is required. The caller retains every ownedSequences root and this bounded cursor through the complete admission acknowledgment, including retained credit, before committing the produced continuation; cancellation releases through the shared arena protocol, never by dropping an unacknowledged handle. Event work is bounded control work, separate from admission allocation and literal ownership. Template families are strategies, not the archive universe."
}

theorem{
  name: start_preserves_template,
  parameters: [template : Template],
  statement: ((project (start template) template) = template),
  proof: reflexivity{}
}

theorem{
  name: event_work_is_one,
  parameters: [cursor : Cursor],
  statement: ((eventWork cursor) = 1),
  proof: reflexivity{}
}
\end{semanticmodule}
\end{lexlean}
